Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the organelles framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 221. The resulting operator achieves bound O(671/n^2).

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the organelles decomposition. By minimality, the element a^221 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 671-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

This completes the proof. QED.
